# Merge current develop while retaining predicate guarantees

This ExecPlan is maintained according to `.agent/PLANS.md`. It records the integration of the latest fetched develop into the existing `GH-0000-lmdb-predicate-guarantees` branch, including semantic conflict decisions and validation. No branch change or unrelated cleanup is authorized.

## Purpose / Big Picture

The branch must retain its predicate guarantee and query execution improvements while incorporating develop's snapshot isolation, recovery, query optimizer, Unicode, and Workbench fixes. A successful result is ordinary merge history on the existing branch, published with a non-force push, preserving the original branch head and incorporating the exact latest fetched develop head. Parent approved a first local checkpoint of the active merge, followed by an ordinary merge of refreshed develop and complete verification before publication. Existing untracked work must remain byte-identical.

## Surprises & Discoveries

- The branch and develop diverge by 404 and 372 commits respectively from merge base `bbb4c0125897568e57b70dc5185faee4e1df4b23`. Incoming develop changes 1,114 paths; 113 overlap branch changes, and a preliminary merge reports 94 conflict paths.
- The required baseline root clean install completed offline in 48.083 seconds with BUILD SUCCESS. Its original full log is preserved at `/tmp/rdf4j-small-things-premerge-clean-install.log`.
- The remote branch equals the starting local head, so no remote branch integration is necessary before merging develop.
- Baseline full evaluation module passes 2,116 tests. The LMDB selection passes except `LmdbOptimizerPipelineTest.lowHeapAutomaticStoreUsesUnifiedEngineAndPageCardinality`, which times out waiting 30 seconds for its child JVM before draining child output. The initial reports and log are retained; a focused unchanged rerun distinguishes baseline persistence.

## Decision Log

- Decision: Use Routine D for a semantic merge, with two non-overlapping workers for shared query and dataset code and a coordinator for LMDB, Maven, formatting, the index, and publication.
  Rationale: Snapshot and optimizer changes compose across many interfaces; a textual resolution alone cannot establish correctness.
  Date/Author: 2026-10-16, Codex.
- Decision: Preserve develop's admitted dataset and snapshot lifetime model as a coherent contract, while porting branch optimizations to those interfaces.
  Rationale: Native query execution must resolve values from the same snapshot it scans; optimization cannot weaken transaction guarantees. Exact details require the current preimages and tests.
  Date/Author: 2026-10-16, Codex.
- Decision: Keep the local optimizer sampling QueryExecutionDeadline active through admission, scanning, value resolution, and condition evaluation. Branch stratification and minimum-evidence attempts remain bounded by that hard deadline; local expiry makes evidence unavailable, while caller expiry, interrupts, and unexpected cancellation propagate.
  Rationale: Develop commit `2539918245` explicitly makes published sampling expiration authoritative, and the configuration advertises `optimizerSamplingMaxMillis`; no reviewed public contract promises a soft extension. Partial strata cannot be published as strong unbiased evidence.
  Date/Author: 2026-10-16, Codex parent milestone review.

## Outcomes & Retrospective

Work is in progress. All 94 textual conflicts are resolved; the merged tree is undergoing compilation and semantic integration validation. The original branch and develop SHAs remain unchanged until publication review.

## Context and Orientation

Work is performed at `/Users/havardottestad/Documents/Programming/rdf4j-small-things`. Starting head is `c8fe5de85447a9efc8aa0eaf5ff65b485b8643f4`; fetched develop is `be37af39da39eaca67633953f41a618d824da9c7`. `core/sail/base` defines datasets and shared transaction lifetimes; `core/sail/lmdb` implements native storage and branch execution accelerators; `core/queryalgebra` contains optimizer and evaluation contracts. The query worker owns queryalgebra, queryparser, queryrender, and common iterator conflict files. The dataset worker owns sail/base, sail/api, repository/sail, federation, and repository/SPARQL testsuites. The coordinator owns all remaining files, index updates, Maven, formatting, and publication.

## Plan of Work

Capture focused baseline tests before starting the actual merge. Inspect both sides of each contract and communicate shared API decisions before LMDB dependent changes. Start `git merge --no-commit --no-ff origin/develop`; resolve conflicts inside assigned paths while keeping optimization behavior and native lifecycle guarantees. Retain tests from both histories, resolve deleted optimizer implementations according to their replacement architecture, and add regression tests only for additional repairs that the integration exposes. Format once after edits settle, inspect every formatter side effect, and run root build plus relevant affected module tests. Parent review occurs before any commit or push. After review, explicitly stage only necessary merge paths, create the merge commit, push only the existing branch, and compare local and remote full SHAs.

## Concrete Steps

The initial build uses `mvn -B -ntp -Dmaven.compiler.showWarnings=false -T 1C -o -Dmaven.repo.local=.m2_repo -Pquick clean install`, preserving the full output in `maven-build.log`. Tests use `python3 .codex/skills/mvnf/scripts/mvnf.py <selection> --retain-logs`, with the selected module specified where needed. Tests never use `-am` or `-q`. Formatting uses the repository copyright checker and `mvn -o -Dmaven.repo.local=.m2_repo -q -T 2C process-resources`. All Maven operations remain serialized.

## Validation and Acceptance

Run existing tests that cover shared query evaluation, dataset lifetimes, and LMDB value/transaction/optimizer behavior both before and after integration. After targeted tests pass, verify the affected modules and appropriate storage integration suites. Preserve exact reports, logs, counts, and limitations; a quick build alone is not correctness proof. Acceptance additionally requires zero unmerged paths, no introduced conflict markers, clean diff whitespace, develop ancestry in the final commit, remote equality after a non-force push, and unchanged original untracked manifest contents.

## Idempotence and Recovery

No reset, clean, stash, restore, force push, branch switch, or deletion of unexpected files is allowed. If Maven lacks cached dependencies, retry the identical command once online and return offline. Preserve report snapshots before subsequent runs overwrite them. A rejected push requires fetching and inspecting remote divergence before an ordinary integration; never force push.

## Artifacts and Notes

The original untracked manifest is `/tmp/rdf4j-small-things-merge-untracked-baseline.json` with 8,877 entries. The preview merge report is `/tmp/rdf4j-small-things-develop-merge-tree.txt`. Initial verify evidence will be written to a previously unused top-level evidence file, with full logs retained separately. These artifacts are not to be staged with the merge.

## Interfaces and Dependencies

No new dependencies or public API changes beyond integrating existing upstream contracts are intended. Shared dataset admission, replay, and value lifetime APIs must be agreed before adapting LMDB-specific execution paths. Existing predicate guarantees and branch optimizations must compose with the retained query optimizer behavior across arbitrary query structure, rather than selecting one side wholesale.
